# Insights — RELL Seas Discord build thinking

Batches: 2026-10-05 S01+S02+S03; 2026-10-05 S04+S05+S06; 2026-10-05 S07+S08+S09; 2026-10-05 FINAL S10+S11+S12. Each card = one distinct piece of user thinking + evidence + volume + server decision.
Evidence quotes are byte-identical to `comments_quality.jsonl` rows (script-extracted). Volumes are counted, not estimated.

## S01 — Release date / window (14 cards)

### INS-001 — Summer window is the emotional deadline
*Confidence: high | evidence: 4 quotes (top likes [165, 47, 42]) | langs: {'en': 4} | merged_rows: 5 | clusters: summer/release/YEAR=223, coming/out/when=437, release/game/when=704*

Users believe RELL Seas must release before/during summer because summer is when they actually have free time to play. The pain is seasonal life plans, not abstract impatience: an athlete faces his last free summer before adulthood, others track "halfway through summer vacation" with no Movie 3 news, and any summer that passes without release feels stolen. When the summer window slips, disappointment converts directly into delay cynicism.

**Discord decision:** Add FAQ + containment: pin a "no manufactured dates, rough windows only with source+slip tag" rule; run a monthly locked-answer prediction ritual to contain date energy instead of banning it.

**Evidence:**

- (25 likes, en, `UgxKqekAmC_4y6Mr1T54AaABAg`, vid `yz4JSysaVcg`) Man i had so much trust in this game releas for 2 years now. I was looking forward to playing it for so much time. Unfortunately this will be my last summer before i become an adult and because I'm an athlete this will probably be my last summer where i am able to play games and have so much free time. When they said the game will be releasing in 2025 around december i was so happy because it was  [...]
- (47 likes, en, `UgyT2addg4qm1JrVTMl4AaABAg`, vid `7dpBZVBSKq0`) Yk what else would be insane? A release date. I almost had a brain aneurysm just thinking about a good roblox game ACTUALLY releasing. We almost halfway through summer vacation and still no news on movie 3.
- (165 likes, en, `Ugw8QEw6jzu6-6WPSrl4AaABAg`, vid `s18PuMlf128`) nah rell seas tryna get us excited again just for movie 3 to not release in summer 😭
- (42 likes, en, `UgzDHlITiArTHMXy1V94AaABAg`, vid `UYODeOH1MV0`) they were just genuinely tickling our butt cheeks from the release of movie 3. they really let the whole summer pass and NOW they record it

*Sources: UgxKqekAmC_4y6Mr1T54AaABAg, UgyT2addg4qm1JrVTMl4AaABAg, Ugw8QEw6jzu6-6WPSrl4AaABAg, UgzDHlITiArTHMXy1V94AaABAg, comment-sentiment-findings.md §4 (summer-window cluster 223 @ 11.5 avg likes; "release before summer, I want to play all summer" 1,963 likes)*

### INS-002 — Fans are aging out while waiting
*Confidence: high | evidence: 3 quotes (top likes [158, 41, 13]) | langs: {'en': 2, 'pt': 1} | merged_rows: 3 | clusters: waiting/years/im=284, release/game/when=704*

Users believe the wait has outlasted whole life chapters: two summers plus high school plus military bootcamp, grandchildren-graduation jokes, and a Portuguese speaker who grew up, got a job and now has TCC work while the devs still "guard sneak for movie 3 that never launches". The fear is that by launch they will have no time left to play. This cohort is a launch-day reactivation reservoir if the server keeps a low-pressure alumni identity.

**Discord decision:** Add reactivation event: "I was here since quarantine" alumni role + comeback events tied to real milestones (M3, EAC), never to invented dates.

**Evidence:**

- (158 likes, en, `UgxxRw8zvETNbKCLu1Z4AaABAg`, vid `WTYEFL9U1zc`) brother, I spent 2 summers, graduated high school, joined the military, graduated bootcamp, Still in the military in A-School and this game has not released, Lol
- (41 likes, en, `Ugwh4g5WvFiF03jmykt4AaABAg`, vid `bHioe6iq4Kw`) video from 2 years ago, game gonna release when my grandchildren graduate
- (13 likes, pt, `Ugx_mw80M_rvPFfjRzZ4AaABAg`, vid `9sbfC_a5dIA`) ah mn essa parada dos rell brothers é sacanagem p krlh, eu tava animado pra jogar esse jogo em 2024 qnd lançaram os dev logs 1 e 2, e o jogo estava perfeitamente jogavel desde então mas ficam querendo adicionar mais e mais coisa antes do jogo lançar. [...]

*Sources: UgxxRw8zvETNbKCLu1Z4AaABAg, Ugwh4g5WvFiF03jmykt4AaABAg, Ugx_mw80M_rvPFfjRzZ4AaABAg, comment-sentiment-findings.md §1.7 (aging-out cluster 265 @ 7.0; uniques 736/1035)*

### INS-003 — Year jokes are grief with a punchline
*Confidence: high | evidence: 5 quotes (top likes [116, 83, 54]) | langs: {'en': 3, 'pt': 1, 'es': 1} | merged_rows: 5 | clusters: YEAR/release/seas=484, here/YEAR/not=261, year/release/game=236, next/update/year=245*

Users cope with datelessness by joking the game releases in 2027/2028/2043/2048 or after GTA 6, calling devs "delayvelopers" and the game "the GTA 6 of Roblox". The jokes carry a real verdict: trust is spent ("never gonna get hyped even when they say the release date"), and the humor crosses languages intact (PT December-promise cycle, ES excuse-communique prediction). Suppressing the jokes would flood serious channels; containing them preserves signal.

**Discord decision:** Add containment channel: meme/salt channel with rules (seasposting model) + a "Delay Tax" bot joke (+365 days per "when release") so salt stays out of feedback channels.

**Evidence:**

- (116 likes, en, `UgzlMCEa40fvoBX9Cp14AaABAg`, vid `n_aVdvuRbXs`) Gave up on rell seas in 2024 twin, I really respect yall for holding out this long 😭🙏 GTA 6 gonna keep you guys busy until release of 2028 💀

- (54 likes, en, `Ugw49WNanKi6PweyVYF4AaABAg`, vid `7ebIrW1Lrbc`) Yeah this is the gta 6 of robox, I'm never gonna get hyed even when they say the release date cuz  I'm sure they re gonna delay it again and again

### INS-004 — Timeline audits destroyed date credibility
*Confidence: high | evidence: 4 quotes (top likes [38, 22, 5]) | langs: {'en': 3, 'pt': 1} | merged_rows: 4 | clusters: date/release/movie=401, release/game/when=704, never/game/rell=238*

Users keep receipts: Trello dates slid February to April to summer to November; "on track for 2024" became "85-90% done in 2026"; a summer-2024 promise collides with claims development started in 2024. They read this as blatant lies rather than slips, and suspect membership-farming ("Something monumental is in motion since 2021"). Portuguese voices add the structural theory: the old team never polished, everything was redone with a smaller team. Sourced audits are the most-respected post format observed.

**Discord decision:** Add living sourced timeline: pinned timeline quoting rough windows verbatim with source + "can slip" tag; never convert to exact dates; publish a dev-accountability tracker (quote vs reality).

**Evidence:**

### INS-005 — Announcing early was the original sin
*Confidence: high | evidence: 3 quotes (top likes [88, 51, 47]) | langs: {'en': 3} | merged_rows: 3 | clusters: please/release/rell=438, released/game/not=283*

Users believe the devs should have kept the game private until 60-70% done instead of promising December 2024 when development commenced in 2023. They demand consistency and accountability: what did gold/silver subscribers actually get beyond early sneak peeks, and where is the video addressing community complaints. Others just want the version churn to stop ("making more versions over and over") and the movie to stop outranking the game in community attention.

**Discord decision:** Add tone law + FAQ: project-manager voice (progress bars, phases), no hype-man "soon"; archive old promises (subs, windows) in a public promise-archive so members stop re-litigating them.

I don't know man it seems like the game might not get released until late 2027/ early 2028 based on the information we are getting. I just wish they were more consistent, and clear with their words. Like if the [...]
- (47 likes, en, `Ugz4c6GJkX4tkMdwW8x4AaABAg`, vid `l2We31Bj3-w`) They just need to release the game. They just keep making more versions of the game over and over and over.

### INS-006 — Forgetting the game is folk therapy
*Confidence: medium | evidence: 4 quotes (top likes [55, 36, 10]) | langs: {'en': 1, 'pt': 3} | merged_rows: 4 | clusters: waiting/years/im=284, cant/wait/play=289*

A cross-language wisdom tradition tells sufferers to stop waiting: fixed-date-less waiting is "terrible for the mind", so forget RELL Seas until launch, then judge what it actually is instead of entering with "esperei anos pra isso" defect-hunting. One Portuguese speaker left the Discord entirely and will only return at launch; another reports feeling nothing at news anymore and will watch Movie 3 "like any video". Numbness is churn risk disguised as peace.

**Discord decision:** Add low-pressure alumni role + "sneaks-appreciation" fallback culture so disengaged fans can idle without leaving; comeback events only on real milestones.

### INS-007 — Every date is priced as a future delay
*Confidence: high | evidence: 4 quotes (top likes [150, 39, 25]) | langs: {'en': 4} | merged_rows: 4 | clusters: release/game/when=704, releasing/game/never=307*

Users now treat any official date as the setup for the next delay: they joke about betting a parlay on the post-date delay, warn hype will "fizzle out depending how far open release is delayed", flinch at the words "release date", and decode "soon" as three months plus a 2027 release. This is the hype-to-salt cycle in miniature: pride flips to salt within one dateless drop. The server must never manufacture dates.

**Discord decision:** Add monthly prediction ritual with locked answers + post-release reveal party; quote windows verbatim with source + slip tag; slowmode hype spikes.

**Evidence:**

- (25 likes, en, `Ugz0bbMWcVJBlI3dJC54AaABAg.A_zAbERS18MA_zQUADlYne`, vid `dhfGEhFivrM`) Lets bet on the game getting delayed again after they give an official release date. Im ready for my Parlay.
- (25 likes, en, `UgzHsfLX_H2ayPOZFS54AaABAg`, vid `xWpDVh6jUkw`) Hype is going to fizzle out like crazy depending on how far the open release is delayed
- (150 likes, en, `UgxFeUM_ylvDEr1BG7p4AaABAg`, vid `YOM5fZ3BNqE`) I swear if I hear the words release date I’m gonna cry for the next 5 minutes
- (39 likes, en, `Ugwn9_UHYiNJ8vt92lN4AaABAg`, vid `IlS3B8A3_k8`) summary: movie 3 isn't "soon" he means as in 3 months from now and the actual release got delayed till 2027

*Sources: Ugz0bbMWcVJBlI3dJC54AaABAg.A_zAbERS18MA_zQUADlYne, UgzHsfLX_H2ayPOZFS54AaABAg, UgxFeUM_ylvDEr1BG7p4AaABAg, Ugwn9_UHYiNJ8vt92lN4AaABAg, comment-sentiment-findings.md §4 (hype-to-salt cycle is days, not weeks); reddit-threads.md A4/A11 (prediction rituals)*

### INS-008 — Begging humor is a pressure valve
*Confidence: medium | evidence: 4 quotes (top likes [252, 107, 61]) | langs: {'en': 3, 'pt': 1} | merged_rows: 4 | clusters: YEAR/release/seas=484, never/game/rell=238*

Users plead through jokes: a grandma with two months to live must experience the "aura", excuses for a 2027 push will be shinier tiger-boss hair, and November will bring a communiqué of 12,1000-hour workdays guaranteeing 2027. Roger laughing at Laugh Tale is retconned as laughing at the release date. The jokes are affectionate, not hostile, and punishing them would read as punching down.

**Discord decision:** No action against the jokes beyond containment channel routing; optionally run meme contests with game-lore prompts to harvest the creativity.

### INS-009 — Outsiders beg to be let in as-is
*Confidence: medium | evidence: 3 quotes (top likes [65, 43, 1]) | langs: {'en': 3} | merged_rows: 3 | clusters: please/release/rell=438, play/game/when=378*

Migrant and casual voices plead from outside: one fleeing Blox Fruits toxicity (bullying, P2W, no PvP toggle) hopes Seas comes soon; one just wants the game already for a homeless mother; a third says the community would take the game with glitches since no launch is perfect. This is demand for a playable imperfect game over a perfect unreleased one, and a migrant-onboarding opportunity.

**Discord decision:** Add Blox Fruits migrant onboarding ("coming from Blox Fruits? start here": slower tactical combat, no-bullying norms) + EAC survival guides for day-one players.

### INS-010 — Ship it, then update weekly
*Confidence: medium | evidence: 3 quotes (top likes [68, 6, 0]) | langs: {'en': 3} | merged_rows: 3 | clusters: releasing/game/never=307, dropping/seas/never=266*

A vocal faction believes the release blockers are self-imposed: two tester tiers plus a whole movie before dropping means "not till 2027 summer", so release the game and iterate with weekly updates on player feedback instead. One gave up because "supposed to come out last year" with only clips to show. This thinking directly conflicts with the perfectionist stance and will explode at EAC if testers feel the wait bought bugginess anyway.

**Discord decision:** Add EAC trust kit now: day-one survival guides, "what testers wish they knew", bug-report templates, explicit paid-vs-free difference tables; position server as tester-friendly before EAC lands.

### INS-011 — Pro-delay minority trusts the polish
*Confidence: medium | evidence: 4 quotes (top likes [277, 103, 43]) | langs: {'en': 3, 'fr': 1} | merged_rows: 4 | clusters: release/game/when=704*

A minority defends the wait: getting impatient is "dumb" when devs said they could have released long ago but chose quality for fans; others are glad delays bought lockpicking, scaling cooking and real progression instead of chest-opening; a French voice says rushing fixes nothing on Roblox and to let them work without pressure. Taking breaks or delaying is "okay" because the grind never stops. This is the server's tone anchor: quote it back when salt peaks.

**Discord decision:** Add feedback loop ritual: "You asked, it is in" posts quoting pro-polish voices + dev work-ethic spotlights to revive dead rooms without hype-man language.

### INS-012 — Dateless drops feel incomplete
*Confidence: high | evidence: 3 quotes (top likes [315, 12, 1]) | langs: {'en': 3} | merged_rows: 4 | clusters: date/release/movie=401, trailer/movie/date=340*

Users report a film that felt "strange" and incomplete in a way older trailers, dev logs and Movies did not: everyone expected something big that would shock other developers, even teasers did it better, and withholding a date made the game feel less hyped, a "terrible decision". Others name the wound plainly: disappointed by lack of communication with release now "99.9% next year", windows come and go with no clear date. Date-shaped content with no date is the trigger.

**Discord decision:** Add content rule: label every drop honestly (sneak-peek vs Movie vs news); never attach date-shaped framing without a sourced window + slip tag; staff visible in chat after drops.

## S02 — Movie 3 / EAC / testing (15 cards)

### INS-015 — "Open testing" reads as dishonest
*Confidence: high | evidence: 5 quotes (top likes [4696, 2264, 417]) | langs: {'en': 5} | merged_rows: 5 | clusters: tester/become/give=255*

Users reject the label outright at massive scale: "open testing is limited to applicants, that is not open testing", "just closed testing phase 2", "what are yall smoking". They cite dictionary definitions (open = accessible to everyone) and note it is closed testing available only to those who pay. One adds the killer detail: paid open testing is just closed testing with payment as an option; real open testing would be for all Roblox players. This is the single biggest trust wound in the corpus.

**Discord decision:** Add permanent plain-language EAC/CC explainer (biggest FAQ win): decision-tree graphic sub-history to EAC to launch; rename-guard rule: staff never say "open" for gated access.

- (45 likes, en, `UgyQUq1RAb1W8jhtsoR4AaABAg.A93cM574L7pA93lKRIczgn`, vid `ZCzdKbmQerg`) @pimpincodax8128 If I'm not mistaken, open testing means it's accessible to everyone.

Users frame payment-gated testing as monetized early access, not testing: "closed paid testing", with comparisons to games "accused of scams like 10 times" for the same model. ### INS-021 — "Soon" now means years, not weeks
*Confidence: high | evidence: 4 quotes (top likes [119, 39, 25]) | langs: {'en': 4} | merged_rows: 5 | clusters: movie/release/not=572, releasing/game/never=307*

"Soon" is a dead word: "Movie 3 Soon (ten more years)" gets grateful laughs, "soon" is decoded as three months plus a 2027 release, and two years of "coming soon" is itself the joke. Trello archaeology (February to April to summer to November) proves each "soon" slid a quarter. The community begs for the word to be retired in favor of phases. This is the tone-law card for all staff comms.

**Discord decision:** Add tone law: ban "soon/coming soon" in staff comms; use "In Progress/Phase X" + progress-tracker channel (combat/map/optimization bars) instead of words.

### INS-033 — Optimization is the success criterion

The receipts offered are technical: occlusion culling so only visible things load, "even loads on a Samsung fridge", built for most devices. Technical proof beats adjectives.

*Confidence: high | evidence: 7 quotes (top likes [35, 16, 7]) | langs: {'en': 3, 'pt': 2, 'fr': 2} | merged_rows: 7 | clusters: free/game/play=239*

### INS-063 — Trade and market demand
*Confidence: medium | evidence: 5 quotes (top likes [626, 339, 201]) | langs: {'pt': 2, 'en': 2, 'fr': 1} | merged_rows: 5 | clusters: *

Acquisition must be fair and legible: a Portuguese voice warns the "enzos" grinding spins, infinite quests, infinite trades and PvP button-spam "will quit NA HORA"; a second relays King Legacy UX lessons (repeatable missions, fruit-to-hand purchases, hourly spins); GPO's 0.5% fruits plus a $100 notifier gamepass show RNG married to paywalled information; and a magnet pity (275-day average, 334-day pity, 2-week events) shows transparency done right; a French jungler keeps any fruit found because "c'est vraiment rare". With the anti-rarity essay warning RNG rarity plus tradeability breeds black markets (INS-055), a fair official market plus published pity is harm reduction.

**Discord decision:** Add fruit-rarity & trade board (embed template: Fruit/Rarity/Owner/Offer) plus published-pity demand to absorb trade spam and undercut black markets.

### INS-121 — Overhype is a diagnosed disease
*Confidence: high | evidence: 3 quotes (top likes [80, 22, 15]) | langs: {'en': 3} | merged_rows: 3 | clusters: *

Players diagnose the hype cycle itself: Seas seen as "perfect, impossible to conquer" on "0 knowledge of how it plays"; hype "dies down every time devs deliver a promise then switch last second"; and the original sin — "never should have announced so early... dying of hype will kill more than lack of money... moving on". Overhype is framed as lethal, not flattering. Under-promise discipline is survival.

**Discord decision:** Add hype-discipline norms (evidence-first, slowmode on spikes, no manufactured dates); early-announcement postmortem pinned as caution.
